# golden inputs: the synthetic table, the pin of the AS7772 table and the scripts ("== script <id> <tag>"), body lines prefixed ">".
# Golden C++ side of the MACsec end-to-end test at MDIO level (crates/sai-macsec-phy/tests/e2e.rs): per script of SAI
# object operations the output of sai-macsec's tests/cpp/driver.cpp built with -DSAI_MACSEC_DRIVER_CHIP (the fake
# backend's chip variant: a register file, FIFO registers, dead PHYs; SAI_MACSEC_DRIVER_PORTS=32,
# SAI_MACSEC_DRIVER_DELAYS=1) over the frontend's sai_macsec.cpp + sai_extmacsec.cpp + CORE: per operation the SAI
# status, the values read, the diag status text and every MDIO bus access in order (reads with the value read,
# writes with the value written, delays, refused accesses). Two variants, as the test uses them: the UNMODIFIED
# C++ ("plain") and the C++ with the owner's fixes D9 / D10 ("fixed": sai-macsec's listed_fixes.rs applied to
# sai_macsec.cpp by anchors). The Rust path (sai-macsec + sai-macsec-phy + phy-seq on a recording bus) is held to
# "fixed" to the end of every script; where "fixed" differs from "plain" the script reached D9 / D10.

# Produced by the C++ drivers' own output, via the ignored capture test of this file (it writes both files of this
# golden without their headers; the headers are written by hand):
#   SAI_MACSEC_CAPTURE_DIR=<dir> SAI_MACSEC_E2E_TABLE=<as7772 table> \
#     cargo test -p sai-macsec-phy --test e2e -- --ignored capture_golden_from_the_cpp
# inside rust/sai's package environment (nix/package.nix; SAI_OPENBCM_SRC = ai_re/nix/packages/sai-teralynx/frontend/src).
# Inputs, all in golden/e2e-inputs.txt: 4 backbone scripts + the D9 script + 64 random scripts on the synthetic
# table (stored there; seeds 20261126 .. 20261189, longest random part 80, "syn-rnd-<seed>"), and 4 backbones +
# 48 random scripts on the AS7772 table (seeds 20261226 .. 20261273, random part 60, "as-rnd-<seed>"; the
# backbones carry the read-back words of the table). The AS7772 table is NOT stored (Nix generates it); it is
# pinned by length and FNV-1a 64 ("pinned as7772 ..."), and the AS7772 cases run only when SAI_MACSEC_E2E_TABLE
# names a file with those (else SKIPPED, said on stderr):
#   nixosConfigurations.as7772.config.services.sonic.sai.profile.SAI_OPENBCM_EXT_MACSEC_FILE
#   /nix/store/rqf3pg8g5rklyi5cvx3xg9pdq4sqs6ga-as7772-ext-macsec.tbl
#   sha256 1ef74ead737f7e0189a101e63735b34d4a85968c459ce7383246a3a815aff5c7   (pinned as7772 347896 828b50edfc2b3672)
# The stored inputs are the truth: 'the_golden_inputs_are_the_fixed_corpus' checks that the generators of the test
# (seeds 20261126.. via tests/common/seeded.rs, proptest 1.12.0 ChaCha) still make them.
# Outputs are normalised as the test does (the longest-run-time column of a status port line is replaced by "?"),
# then digested: <lines>:<FNV-1a 64 of the normalised text, with each line's "\n">. Full texts are kept for the 8
# backbones, the D9 case (named-d9) and one random script that reaches D10 (syn-rnd-20261141) as
# "== text <id> <plain|fixed>" sections, lines prefixed ">"; where plain equals fixed only "fixed" is kept.
# NOT to be regenerated from the Rust output (the C++ goes away at H5; regenerating from Rust would make the test
# compare Rust with itself). A deliberate behaviour change edits the affected lines by hand, with a
# "# changed <date>: <reason>" line here.
# Proof that the golden is what the live C++ printed (at capture, 2026-11-26): 'the_golden_equals_the_live_cpp'
# (121 scripts: plain + fixed digests equal, the 10 kept texts equal, "listed" equal), next to the 7 live tests.
# Negative controls: 'the_golden_notices_a_change'.
